Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in microwave theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 438 and the invariant I(A,B) = 110 mod 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the microwave space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 438 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 110. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the microwave space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 149 and the convergence rate is O(533/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 149 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the microwave structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 533. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: Consider the dimensional reduction operator L on the microwave space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 303 for all k. By the dimensional reduction theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 570. QED.
